def sha(b): return hashlib.sha256(b).hexdigest()
def require(v,m):
 if not v: raise ValueError(m)
def mod(name,b,p):
 m=types.ModuleType(name);m.__file__=str(p);sys.modules[name]=m
 exec(compile(b.decode('utf-8-sig'),str(p),'exec'),m.__dict__);return m
def rpy(roll,pitch,yaw):
 cr,sr,cp,sp,cy,sy=math.cos(roll),math.sin(roll),math.cos(pitch),math.sin(pitch),math.cos(yaw),math.sin(yaw)
 return [[cy*cp,cy*sp*sr-sy*cr,cy*sp*cr+sy*sr],[sy*cp,sy*sp*sr+cy*cr,sy*sp*cr-cy*sr],[-sp,cp*sr,cp*cr]]

  return dict(label=label,p_world=pp[0].tolist(),wheel_low=g['low'][0].tolist(),wheel_high=g['high'][0].tolist(),lowest_nonwheel_shapes=lowest,

   COM_world=com[0].tolist(),wheel_point_support=pts.tolist(),COM_support_line_distance_m=float(dist),COM_support_fraction=float(fraction),anchor6_m=anchors,all_anchors_under_original2mm=max(anchors)<=.002)

 [~,rr,pivot]=qr(dyn0.loop_J(:,6+dependent).','vector');diagR=abs(diag(rr));
 rankTolerance=1e-9*max([diagR;1]);rankJ=sum(diagR>rankTolerance);
 assert(rankJ==16,'Projected own16 passive-coordinate rank changed; retain error instead of pseudo-lock.');
 ctx.loopRows=pivot(1:rankJ);
 options=optimoptions('fmincon','Display','off','Algorithm','sqp', ...
  'MaxIterations',180,'MaxFunctionEvaluations',6500,'ConstraintTolerance',1e-8, ...
  'FunctionTolerance',1e-10,'OptimalityTolerance',1e-8,'StepTolerance',1e-11);
 modes={'nearest_positive_floor','nearby_maximum_floor_margin'};candidates=cell(2,1);start=x0;
 for k=1:2
  objective=@(x)stanceObjective(ctx,x,k);
  [x,value,flag,solver]=fmincon(objective,start,[],[],[],[],lower,upper,@(x)stanceConstraints(ctx,x),options);
  candidates{k}=evaluateCandidate(ctx,x,lower,upper,flag,value,solver,modes{k},p);
  if k==1&&candidates{k}.all_geometric_checks_passed,start=x;end
 end
 report=struct('schema','OWN_MOD4_NEARBY_STANCE_FLOOR_SOURCE_V1', ...
  'status','RETURNED_NEARBY_STANCE_CANDIDATES_NOT_DYNAMIC_OR_PHYSX_TEACHER', ...
  'producer_pid',feature('getpid'),'inputs',inputs,'named_joint_order',{names}, ...
  'actuation_order6',{order},'motor_center_is_actual_initial4',q0(act(1:4)), ...
  'motor_neighborhood_radius_rad',.15,'variable_q_indices20',variable,'dependent_indices16',dependent, ...
  'frozen_wheel_spin_and_guide_q_indices10',setdiff(1:30,variable),'q0_actual_initial',q0, ...
  'search_lower23',lower,'search_upper23',upper,'no_actual_q_clamp',true, ...
  'solver_loop_independent_rows',ctx.loopRows,'solver_loop_rank',rankJ,'solver_loop_rank_tolerance',rankTolerance, ...
  'actual150_terminal_state_derived',terminal,'actual150_floor_FK',D.actual150_terminal_exact_transform_FK, ...
  'actual150_original_cached_nonwheel_force_is_zero',terminal.physical_arguments.nonwheel_max_N==0, ...
  'candidates',{candidates},'floor_margin1mm_is_planning_preference_not_new_PhysX_guard',true, ...
  'step_riser_proxy_not_optimized_or_relaxed',true,'original2mm_per_environment_unchanged',true, ...
  'no_reset_root_force_velocity_or_plant_state_write',true,'candidate_is_6motor_target_not_instant_pose_write',true, ...
  'dynamic_transition_PD_delay_DR_lean_torque_contact_stability_unproven',true, ...
  'physical_teacher_valid',false,'runtime_admission',false);
catch error
 report=struct('schema','OWN_MOD4_NEARBY_STANCE_FLOOR_SOURCE_V1','status','ORIGINAL_NEARBY_STANCE_SOURCE_ERROR_RETAINED', ...
  'producer_pid',feature('getpid'),'inputs',inputs,'message',error.message,'identifier',error.identifier, ...
  'stack',error.stack,'full_report',getReport(error,'extended','hyperlinks','off'),'physical_teacher_valid',false);
end
writeNewJSON4A(fullfile(outDir,'OWN_MOD4_NEARBY_STANCE_FLOOR_SOURCE_V1_ACTUAL.json'),report);
end

function s=stanceState(ctx,x)
q=ctx.q0;q(ctx.variable)=x(1:20);R=rz4A(ctx.yaw)*ry(x(21))*rx(x(22));
g=own_mod4_fk_teacher_v1(ctx.C,ctx.G,ctx.names,q,zeros(3,1),R);
p=[ctx.xy;ctx.floorZ-mean(g.wheel_low(3,:))];
g=own_mod4_fk_teacher_v1(ctx.C,ctx.G,ctx.names,q,p,R);
wheel=[find(strcmp(g.body_names,'left_wheel')),find(strcmp(g.body_names,'right_wheel'))];
keep=~ismember(g.shape_body_index,wheel);clearance=g.shape_low(3,keep)-ctx.floorZ;
points=wheelSupportPoints(ctx.G,g.body_names,g.body_position,g.body_rotation);
[distance,fraction]=supportLine(g.COM,points);
s=struct('q',q,'R',R,'p',p,'g',g,'clearance',clearance(:),'shape_indices',find(keep), ...
 'support_points',points,'support_distance',distance,'support_fraction',fraction, ...
 'state',struct('p',p,'R',R,'v',zeros(3,1),'w',zeros(3,1),'q',q,'dq',zeros(30,1)));
end

function value=stanceObjective(ctx,x,mode)
deviation=[(x(1:20)-ctx.center(1:20))./ctx.scale;(x(21:22)-ctx.center(21:22))/.2];
if mode==1,value=sum(deviation.^2)+1e-4*(x(23)/.05)^2;
else,value=1e-3*sum(deviation.^2)-x(23)/.05;end
end

function [c,ceq]=stanceConstraints(ctx,x)
s=stanceState(ctx,x);delta=s.q(ctx.act([1,3]))-s.q(ctx.act([2,4]));
c=[1000*(x(23)-s.clearance);-1.0843-delta;delta-.85;-.0-s.support_fraction;s.support_fraction-1];
ceq=[1000*s.g.constraint_vector(ctx.loopRows);1000*(s.g.wheel_low(3,1)-s.g.wheel_low(3,2));1000*s.support_distance];
end

function candidate=evaluateCandidate(ctx,x,lb,ub,flag,value,solver,mode,p)
s=stanceState(ctx,x);[c,ceq]=stanceConstraints(ctx,x);domain=actualQDomain(ctx.C,ctx.names,s.q,ctx.act);
d=own_mod4_tree_dynamics_v1(ctx.C,ctx.G,ctx.names,s.state);[points,JC]=contactsAndJ(ctx.G,d);
[~,rr,pivot]=qr(d.loop_J(:,7:36).','vector');dd=abs(diag(rr));tol=1e-9*max([dd;1]);rankJ=sum(dd>tol);sel=pivot(1:rankJ);
static=staticEffort(ctx.C,p,ctx.names,s.q,ctx.act,d,d.loop_J(ctx.loopRows,:),JC,[]);
anchor6=vecnorm(s.g.closure_residual);mimic=s.g.mimic_residual;
geometric=flag>0&&isempty(domain.actual_q30_violations)&&isempty(domain.actual_CAD_violations) ...
 &&max(anchor6)<=.002&&max(abs(mimic))<=1e-6&&min(s.clearance)>=.001-1e-7 ...
 &&max(abs(s.g.wheel_low(3,:)-ctx.floorZ))<=1e-6&&abs(s.support_distance)<=1e-6 ...
 &&s.support_fraction>=0&&s.support_fraction<=1&&max([0;lb-x;x-ub])<=1e-8;
[sortClear,indices]=sort(s.clearance);lowShapes=cell(min(8,numel(indices)),1);
shapes=ctx.G.shapes;if isstruct(shapes),shapes=num2cell(shapes);end
for j=1:numel(lowShapes)
 idx=s.shape_indices(indices(j));row=shapes{idx};lowShapes{j}=struct('shape_index_matlab',idx,'body',row.body,'type',row.type, ...
  'floor_clearance_m',sortClear(j),'low_world',s.g.shape_low(:,idx),'high_world',s.g.shape_high(:,idx));
end
offsets=[-.4;-.2;0;.2;.4];leanGeometry=cell(numel(offsets),1);
for j=1:numel(offsets)
 xx=x;xx(21)=x(21)+offsets(j);v=stanceState(ctx,xx);
 leanGeometry{j}=struct('pitch_offset_rad',offsets(j),'pitch_rad',xx(21),'roll_rad',xx(22), ...
  'hypothetical_point_support_root',v.p,'minimum_nonwheel_floor_m',min(v.clearance), ...
  'COM_support_line_error_m',v.support_distance,'wheel_bottom_height_m',v.g.wheel_low(3,:), ...
  'not_static_equilibrium_or_dynamic_lean_proof',true);
end
candidate=struct('mode',mode,'exitflag',flag,'objective',value,'solver',solver,'x23',x, ...
 'q30_reference_only',s.q,'q4_state_target',s.q(ctx.act(1:4)),'q4_change_from_actualinitial',s.q(ctx.act(1:4))-ctx.q0(ctx.act(1:4)), ...
 'pitch_rad',x(21),'roll_rad',x(22),'root_reference_only',s.p,'R_reference_only',s.R, ...
 'epigraph_floor_margin_m',x(23),'minimum_nonwheel_floor_clearance_m',min(s.clearance), ...
 'lowest_shapes',{lowShapes},'wheel_low_world',s.g.wheel_low,'wheel_high_world',s.g.wheel_high, ...
 'COM_world',s.g.COM,'COM_line_distance_m',s.support_distance,'COM_support_fraction',s.support_fraction, ...
 'all6_anchor_m',anchor6,'all_mimic_residual',mimic,'actual_q_domain',domain, ...
 'max_solver_constraint_inequality',max([0;c]),'max_solver_constraint_equality_scaled',max(abs(ceq)), ...
 'all_geometric_checks_passed',geometric,'full_loop_rank_at_candidate',rankJ,'diagnostic_full_rank_rows',sel, ...
 'static_loop_rows',ctx.loopRows,'static_basis_from_projected_initial_not_nonzero_phi_rank_inflation',true, ...
 'gas_actual_source_nominal',{d.gas},'static_solution',static,'hypothetical_lean_geometry_only',{leanGeometry}, ...
 'all_state_joint_bounds_and_PD_target_domains_are_distinct',true, ...
 'candidate_to_original6motor_control_requires_NEW_PhysX_transition_validation',true, ...
 'physical_teacher_valid',false);
end

% Exact own V4A static/PD/contact/helpers, originalSHA a1e842661ec205c4bab0185fb41b6bb5a346a041048780de5b26ecb0448df609
function [distance, fraction] = supportLine(COM, points)
line = points(1:2, 2)-points(1:2, 1);
offset = COM(1:2)-points(1:2, 1);
assert(norm(line) > .1, 'Own two wheel transverse support line required.');
distance = (line(2)*offset(1)-line(1)*offset(2))/norm(line);
fraction = (offset.'*line)/(line.'*line);
end

function solution = staticEffort(C, p, names, q, act, d, J, JC, fixedTorque)
r = size(J, 1); n = 6+r+6;
E = [d.B_action_torque_order6, J.', JC.'];
b = d.h-d.gas_generalized_force;
peak = [C.leg_motor.peak_torque_nm*ones(4, 1); C.wheel_motor.peak_torque_nm*ones(2, 1)];
continuous = [C.leg_motor.continuous_torque_nm*ones(4, 1); C.wheel_motor.continuous_torque_nm*ones(2, 1)];
lb = [-peak; -inf(r+6, 1)]; ub = [peak; inf(r+6, 1)];
if ~isempty(fixedTorque)
    lb(1:6) = fixedTorque; ub(1:6) = fixedTorque;
end
for wheel = 1:2, lb(6+r+(wheel-1)*3+3) = 0; end
A = []; rhs = []; mu = .7;
for wheel = 1:2
    idx = 6+r+(wheel-1)*3+(1:3);
    for sx = [-1, 1]
        for sy = [-1, 1]
            row = zeros(1, n); row(idx) = [sx, sy, -mu];
            A = [A; row]; rhs = [rhs; 0]; %#ok<AGROW>
        end
    end
end
for j = 1:4
    % Position targets may exceed a named JOINT STATE limit intentionally.
    % Only original raw +/-6 and CAD target projection constrain this solve.
    row = zeros(1, n); row(j) = 1/60;
    A = [A; row; -row]; rhs = [rhs; 3-q(act(j)); 3+q(act(j))]; %#ok<AGROW>
end
for j = [1, 3]
    row = zeros(1, n); row(j) = 1/60; row(j+1) = -1/60;
    delta = q(act(j))-q(act(j+1));
    A = [A; row; -row]; rhs = [rhs; p.diff_max-delta; delta-p.diff_min]; %#ok<AGROW>
end
for j = 5:6
    row = zeros(1, n); row(j) = 1/.6;
    A = [A; row; -row]; rhs = [rhs; 60; 60]; %#ok<AGROW>
end
[~, RR, pivot] = qr(E.', 'vector'); diagonal = abs(diag(RR));
eqTolerance = 1e-10*max([diagonal; 1]); eqRank = sum(diagonal > eqTolerance);
selectedEq = pivot(1:eqRank);
H = diag([1./peak.^2; 1e-12*ones(r, 1); 1e-8/(d.total_mass_kg*9.81)^2*ones(6, 1)]);
options = optimoptions('quadprog', 'Display', 'off', 'Algorithm', 'interior-point-convex', ...
    'MaxIterations', 400, 'ConstraintTolerance', 1e-9, 'OptimalityTolerance', 1e-9);
[z, value, flag, solver] = quadprog(H, zeros(n, 1), A, rhs, E(selectedEq, :), b(selectedEq), lb, ub, [], options);
hardFlag = flag; hardSolver = solver; usedSoft = false;
if isempty(z) || flag <= 0 || max(abs(E*z-b)) > 1e-6
    % A bounded least-residual diagnostic is preserved as a failure to prove
    % static equilibrium. It is never relabelled as an equality solution.
    scale = ones(36, 1)/40;
    scale(1:3) = 1/(d.total_mass_kg*9.81);
    scale(4:6) = 1/(d.total_mass_kg*9.81*.3);
    for j = 1:30
        if strcmp(C.joints.(names{j}).type, 'slide'), scale(6+j) = 1/(d.total_mass_kg*9.81); end
    end
    W2 = diag(scale.^2);
    softH = E.'*W2*E+1e-8*H;
    softH = (softH+softH.')/2;
    [z, value, flag, solver] = quadprog(softH, -E.'*W2*b, A, rhs, [], [], lb, ub, [], options);
    usedSoft = true;
end
if isempty(z) || any(~isfinite(z))
    solution = struct('status', 'NO_STATIC_OR_BOUNDED_RESIDUAL_SOLUTION', ...
        'hard_exitflag', hardFlag, 'hard_solver', hardSolver, ...
        'last_exitflag', flag, 'last_solver', solver, 'physical_teacher_valid', false);
    return;
end
tau = z(1:6); lambda = z(7:6+r); forces = reshape(z(7+r:end), 3, 2);
residual = E*z-b;
pd = mapTorqueThroughOriginalPD(C, p, q, act, tau);
mappedResidual = d.B_action_torque_order6*pd.actual_torque6_after_nominal_two_signed_clips+J.'*lambda+JC.'*forces(:)-b;
maxConstraintViolation = max([0; A*z-rhs; lb-z; z-ub]);
isEquality = ~usedSoft && flag > 0 && max(abs(residual)) <= 1e-6 ...
    && max(abs(mappedResidual)) <= 1e-6 && maxConstraintViolation <= 1e-6;
status = 'BOUNDED_LEAST_RESIDUAL_NOT_A_STATIC_EQUILIBRIUM_PROOF';
if isEquality, status = 'ALTERNATE_POINT_CONTACT_STATIC_EQUATIONS_SATISFIED_NOT_PHYSX_OR_STABILITY'; end
solution = struct('status', status, 'hard_exitflag', hardFlag, 'hard_solver', hardSolver, ...
    'used_soft_residual_fallback', usedSoft, 'last_exitflag', flag, 'last_solver', solver, ...
    'objective', value, 'tau6_Nm', tau, 'loop_lambda_selected', lambda, ...
    'ground_reaction_world_N', forces, 'mu_assumed_not_measured', mu, ...
    'static_generalized_residual36', residual, 'root_force_residual_N', residual(1:3), ...
    'root_moment_residual_Nm', residual(4:6), 'joint_generalized_residual30', residual(7:36), ...
    'max_mixed_unit_residual', max(abs(residual)), ...
    'all36_residual_after_original_PD_and_clips', mappedResidual, ...
    'static_linear_system_rank', eqRank, 'selected_equality_rows', selectedEq, ...
    'equality_rank_numerical_tolerance', eqTolerance, 'static_matrix_singular_values', svd(E), ...
    'unbounded_nullspace_consistency_residual', b-E*(pinv(E)*b), ...
    'max_inequality_or_bound_violation', maxConstraintViolation, 'peak_effort_limit6_Nm', peak, ...
    'remaining_peak_effort6_Nm', peak-abs(tau), 'continuous_rating6_Nm', continuous, ...
    'within_continuous_rating6', abs(tau) <= continuous, ...
    'continuous_rating_is_not_the_original_runtime_clip', true, ...
    'nominal_PD_target', pd, 'all_velocities_zero', true, ...
    'steady_queue_required_before_claiming_PD_torque', true, ...
    'no_duration_thermal_or_dynamic_stability_proof', true, 'physical_teacher_valid', false);
end

function result = mapTorqueThroughOriginalPD(C, p, q, act, torque)
leg = q(act(1:4))+torque(1:4)/60;
wheel = torque(5:6)/.6;
requestedTarget = [leg; wheel];
rawBefore = [leg/.5; wheel/10];
raw = max(-6, min(6, rawBefore));
processed = [.5*raw(1:4); 10*raw(5:6)];
for j = [1, 3]
    processed(j) = min(processed(j+1)+p.diff_max, max(processed(j+1)+p.diff_min, processed(j)));
end
requested = [60*(processed(1:4)-q(act(1:4))); .6*processed(5:6)];
limit = [C.leg_motor.peak_torque_nm*ones(4, 1); C.wheel_motor.peak_torque_nm*ones(2, 1)];
stall = [C.leg_motor.envelope_intercept_nm*ones(4, 1); C.wheel_motor.envelope_intercept_nm*ones(2, 1)];
speed = [C.leg_motor.no_load_speed_rpm*2*pi/60*ones(4, 1); C.wheel_motor.no_load_speed_rpm*2*pi/60*ones(2, 1)];
[first, clip1] = dc_motor_signed_clip_from210_v1(requested, zeros(6, 1), limit, stall, speed);
[actual, clip2] = dc_motor_signed_clip_from210_v1(first, zeros(6, 1), limit, stall, speed);
outside = [];
for j = 1:4
    limits = C.joints.(pActionName(j)).limits_si;
    if processed(j) < limits(1) || processed(j) > limits(2), outside(end+1) = j; end %#ok<AGROW>
end
result = struct('requested_equivalent_torque6_Nm', torque, 'qtarget_equals_q_plus_tau_over_kp4', requestedTarget(1:4), ...
    'wheel_velocity_target2_rad_s', wheel, 'raw6_before_original_clip', rawBefore, ...
    'raw6_after_original_clip', raw, 'processed_target6_after_CAD', processed, ...
    'actual_torque6_after_nominal_two_signed_clips', actual, 'actual_minus_equivalent_torque6', actual-torque, ...
    'nominal_episode_step_scales6', ones(6, 1), 'two_clip_origin_captured210_not72_runtime_equivalence', true, ...
    'first_clip', clip1, 'second_clip', clip2, 'target_outside_named_q_domain_indices', outside, ...
    'target_outside_named_q_domain_is_not_actual_q_limit_violation', true, ...
    'deliberate_PD_saturation_is_possible_and_must_be_reported', true, ...
    'state_not_clamped_or_modified', true, 'no_external_force', true);
end

function name = pActionName(j)
names = {'left_crank', 'left_hip', 'right_crank', 'right_hip'};
name = names{j};
end

function domain = actualQDomain(C, names, q, act)
violations = struct('joint', {}, 'q', {}, 'limits', {});
for j = 1:30
    lim = C.joints.(names{j}).limits_si;
    if ~isfinite(q(j)) || (~isempty(lim) && (q(j) < lim(1) || q(j) > lim(2)))
        violations(end+1) = struct('joint', names{j}, 'q', q(j), 'limits', lim); %#ok<AGROW>
    end
end
cad = [];
for j = [1, 3]
    delta = q(act(j))-q(act(j+1));
    if delta < -1.0843 || delta > .85, cad(end+1) = j; end %#ok<AGROW>
end
domain = struct('actual_q30_violations', violations, 'actual_CAD_violations', cad, ...
    'no_actual_state_clamp', true, 'actual_q_and_PD_target_domains_are_distinct', true);
end

function [points, JC] = contactsAndJ(G, d)
names = cellstr(string(G.body_names));
points = wheelSupportPoints(G, names, d.body_position, d.body_rotation);
JC = zeros(6, 36);
for j = 1:2
    wheels = {'left_wheel', 'right_wheel'};
    b = find(strcmp(names, wheels{j}), 1);
    offset = points(:, j)-d.body_position(:, b);
    JC((j-1)*3+(1:3), :) = d.body_origin_Jv(:, :, b)-skew(offset)*d.body_Jw(:, :, b);
end
end

function points = wheelSupportPoints(G, bodyNames, P, R)
shapes = G.shapes; if isstruct(shapes), shapes = num2cell(shapes); end
wheelNames = {'left_wheel', 'right_wheel'};
points = zeros(3, 2);
for j = 1:2
    b = find(strcmp(bodyNames, wheelNames{j}), 1);
    ix = find(cellfun(@(s) strcmp(s.body, wheelNames{j}), shapes));
    assert(numel(ix) == 1 && strcmp(shapes{ix}.type, 'Mesh'), 'Own exact single wheel mesh required.');
    shape = shapes{ix};
    vertices = R(:, :, b)*shape.linear_body_from_shape*shape.points.'+P(:, b)+R(:, :, b)*shape.origin_body_m(:);
    z = min(vertices(3, :));
    points(:, j) = mean(vertices(:, abs(vertices(3, :)-z) < 1e-12), 2);
end
end


function [value, meta] = readJSON4A(path, expected, key)
[bytes, meta] = readPinned4A(path, expected, key);
value = jsondecode(native2unicode(bytes(:).', 'UTF-8'));
end
function [bytes, meta] = readPinned4A(path, expected, key)
fid = fopen(path, 'rb'); assert(fid >= 0); cleanup = onCleanup(@() fclose(fid)); %#ok<NASGU>
bytes = fread(fid, Inf, '*uint8'); md = java.security.MessageDigest.getInstance('SHA-256');
md.update(typecast(uint8(bytes(:)), 'int8'));
sha = lower(reshape(dec2hex(typecast(md.digest(), 'uint8'), 2).', 1, []));
assert(strcmpi(sha, expected), ['Source/input SHA mismatch: ', key]);
meta = struct('key', key, 'path', path, 'bytes', numel(bytes), 'sha256', sha, 'read_count', 1);
end
function same = samePath4A(a, b)
same = strcmpi(strrep(a, '\', '/'), strrep(b, '\', '/'));
end
function writeNewJSON4A(path, value)
assert(~isfile(path) && ~isfolder(path)); fid = fopen(path, 'wb'); assert(fid >= 0);
cleanup = onCleanup(@() fclose(fid)); %#ok<NASGU>
bytes = [unicode2native(jsonencode(value, 'PrettyPrint', true), 'UTF-8'), uint8(10)];
assert(fwrite(fid, bytes, 'uint8') == numel(bytes));
end
function R = rx(a)
R = [1, 0, 0; 0, cos(a), -sin(a); 0, sin(a), cos(a)];
end
function R = ry(a)
R = [cos(a), 0, sin(a); 0, 1, 0; -sin(a), 0, cos(a)];
end
function R = rz4A(a)
R = [cos(a), -sin(a), 0; sin(a), cos(a), 0; 0, 0, 1];
end
function R = qmat4A(q)
q = q(:)/norm(q); w = q(1); x = q(2); y = q(3); z = q(4);
R = [1-2*(y*y+z*z), 2*(x*y-w*z), 2*(x*z+w*y); ...
     2*(x*y+w*z), 1-2*(x*x+z*z), 2*(y*z-w*x); ...
     2*(x*z-w*y), 2*(y*z+w*x), 1-2*(x*x+y*y)];
end
function S = skew(v)
S = [0, -v(3), v(2); v(3), 0, -v(1); -v(2), v(1), 0];
end

def H(b):return hashlib.sha256(b).hexdigest()
def git(*args,raw=False):
 p=subprocess.run(['git',*args],cwd=G,capture_output=True)
 if p.returncode:raise RuntimeError('git '+args[0]+': '+p.stderr.decode('utf8','replace')[-1600:])

def h(p):return hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
def rep(s,a,b):
    assert s.count(a)==1,(a,s.count(a));return s.replace(a,b)
def put(p,x):
    with p.open('x',encoding='utf-8') as f:json.dump(x,f,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)

            else:shutil.copy2(p,dest)

    with p.open('x',encoding='utf-8') as f:json.dump(x,f,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2,allow_nan=False)
def rep(s,a,b):
    assert s.count(a)==1,(a,s.count(a));return s.replace(a,b)

CONTROLLER='''"""Own calculated static/LQR ground preparation. Original action6 only."""
import math
ORDER=('left_crank','left_hip','right_crank','right_hip','left_wheel_spin','right_wheel_spin')
class GroundLQR:
 def __init__(self,p):self.p=p;self.last=-1
 def step(self,observation):
  p=self.p
  if observation['kind']=='initial_contact_unknown':
   if self.last!=-1:raise ValueError('Bootstrap once')
   names=observation['joint_names'];q=observation['q30']
   actual=[float(q[names.index(n)]) for n in ORDER[:4]]
   if max(abs(a-b) for a,b in zip(actual,p['q4_hold']))>1e-5:raise ValueError('Different original initial motor pose')
   self.last=0
   return dict(raw6=[2*x for x in actual]+[0.,0.],phase='BOOTSTRAP_CONTACT_UNKNOWN',force_and_contact='UNKNOWN',physical_teacher_valid=False)
  o=observation['source_observation'];k=o['global_control']
  if k<=self.last or not 0<k<200:raise ValueError('Exact fresh 200control preparation scope')
  if o['request_active'] or o['original_done']:raise ValueError('Not a pre-request live preparation input')
  self.last=k
  yaw=p['initial_yaw'];ex=(math.cos(yaw),math.sin(yaw));ey=(-math.sin(yaw),math.cos(yaw))
  root=o['actual_root_xyz'];v=o['world_velocity3'];w=o['world_angular_velocity3']
  error=[sum((root[j]-p['initial_xyz'][j])*ex[j] for j in range(2)),sum(v[j]*ex[j] for j in range(2)),o['root_pitch']-p['pitch_eq'],sum(w[j]*ey[j] for j in range(2))]
  common=-sum(g*x for g,x in zip(p['K_tau_per_wheel'],error))
  # Reuse the original prototype yaw feedback only for its measured initial
  # heading; common sagittal gains above are calculated from own M/h/J.
  yawerror=math.atan2(math.sin(o['root_yaw']-yaw),math.cos(o['root_yaw']-yaw))
  yawrate=-2.*yawerror-.5*w[2]
  differential=.6*.4376/(2*.0625)*yawrate
  tau=[p['tau6_static'][4]+common-differential,p['tau6_static'][5]+common+differential]
  roll=.15*(o['root_roll']-p['roll_eq'])+.03*(w[0]*ex[0]+w[1]*ex[1])
  leg=[q+t/60+d for q,t,d in zip(p['q4_hold'],p['tau6_static'][:4],(roll,-roll,-roll,roll))]
  for i in (0,2):leg[i]=min(leg[i+1]+.85,max(leg[i+1]-1.0843,leg[i]))
  dq=o['actual_motor_dq6'];omega=[dq[4+i]+tau[i]/.6 for i in range(2)]
  requested=[2*x for x in leg]+[x/10 for x in omega]
  raw=[max(-6.,min(6.,x)) for x in requested]
  if not all(math.isfinite(x) for x in raw):raise ValueError('Nonfinite calculated action')
  return dict(raw6=raw,phase='OWN_LQR_ACTUAL_INITIAL_GROUND_HOLD',state_error4=error,
   common_torque_increment_Nm=common,requested_nominal_wheel_torque2=tau,
   planned_leg_target4=leg,requested_wheel_speed2=omega,
   nominal_PD_and_motor_gain_not_actual_measured_torque=True,
   actual_original_command_vx=o['actual_command_vx'],source_only_LQR_is_not_PhysX_stability=True,
   raw_saturation6=[abs(a-b)>1e-12 for a,b in zip(raw,requested)],physical_teacher_valid=False,
   no_state_force_reset_or_goal_write=True)
'''
